[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 8. Database management s ystem (DBMS) - Diploma in Programming  (CS2001)]
There is only one OPPE conducted for the course. But there are 2 chances to attempt it.

[TYPE: unordered_list]
[SECTION: 8. Database management s ystem (DBMS) - Diploma in Programming  (CS2001)]
- Students have to mandatorily attend the OPPE on the first date. If you fail in this, you get a chance to reappear the next weekend. So you get 2 chances to attempt the exam.
- If you are absent on the first date, you do not get the reattempt chance.
- If the first date is inconvenient due to a clash with some other engagement, then you can apply for a postponement to the second date providing sufficient proof. If this is accepted, your exam will be moved to the second date but if you fail in this or are absent, you will not get another chance to reattempt. You will have to do it in the subsequent term .

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 10. Programming concepts using Ja va (Diploma in program ming) (CS2005)]
Repeat_OPPE Criteria (w.e.f from Jan 2026 term) [updated on Jan 05, 2026]

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 10. Programming concepts using Ja va (Diploma in program ming) (CS2005)]
Students who were OPPE eligible and received an I_OP in the May 2026 term, and have registered as Repeat OPPE for the Sep 2026 term, will continue to be considered eligible for OPPE in Sep 2026. However, students who were marked OPPE Ineligible with I_OP or I_Both and have registered for the respective course as Repeat OPPE or Repeat OPPE & End Term must regain OPPE eligibility in the Sep 2026 term by submitting the required assignments and OPPE SCT. If OPPE eligibility is not attained, the OPPE will not be scheduled and a U grade will be awarded for the Sep 2026 term.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 10. Programming concepts using Ja va (Diploma in program ming) (CS2005)]
Eligibility to attend the end term exam: Average of the best 5 out of the first 7 weekly assessments (objective and programming) scores >= 40/100 and attending at least one of the 2 quizzes in the center .

[TYPE: paragraph]

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
Repeat_OPPE Criteria (w.e.f from Jan 2026 term) [updated on Jan 05, 2026]

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
Students who were OPPE eligible and received an I_OP in the May 2026 term, and have registered as Repeat OPPE for the Sep 2026 term, will continue to be considered eligible for OPPE in Sep 2026. However, students who were marked OPPE Ineligible with I_OP or I_Both and have registered for the respective course as Repeat OPPE or Repeat OPPE & End Term must regain OPPE eligibility in the Sep 2026 term by submitting the required assignments and OPPE SCT. If OPPE eligibility is not attained, the OPPE will not be scheduled and a U grade will be awarded for the Sep 2026 term.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
Eligibility for OPPE: The average of the first three BPT >=40 /100

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
Students have to mandatorily attend the OPPE on the first date.

[TYPE: unordered_list]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
- If you fail in this, you get a chance to reappear the next weekend. So you get 2 chances to attempt the exam.
- If the first date is inconvenient due to a clash with some other engagement, then you can apply for a postponement to the second date providing sufficient proof. If this is accepted, your exam will be moved to the second date but if you fail/absent in this, you will not get another chance to reattempt. You will have to do it in the subsequent term.
- If you are absent on 20th Dec, you DON'T get another chance on Jan 3rd..

Re OPPE Eligibility:

[TYPE: unordered_list]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
- • Students must score a minimum of 20 marks in the SC OPPE to be eligible for the ReOPPE.
- • Students must remain present in the proctoring G Meet for a minimum of 1 hour 30 minutes.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
NPPE [Syllabus Weeks 1-9]: (This will give you practice to handle the OPPE, but not part of final course grade)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
GAA =  Average of score in Best 9 out of the 10 weekly graded assignments

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
BPTA: Average of the 4 BPT scores

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
QZ1 = score in Quiz I (0, if not attempted)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
OPPE = Score in online remote proctored programming exam

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
F = score in End Term exam

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 11. System commands (Diploma in programming)  (SE2001)]
Final course score T = 0.05 GAA + 0.25 Qz1 + 0.3 OPPE + 0.3F + 0.1 BPTA

Method: Auto Evaluated

Method: Objective
Proctoring: In person at TCS centers
Percentage contribution: Qz1, Qz2

Assessment Type: End Sem - January 10 2027 , F
Method: 100% Objective
Proctoring: In person at TCS centers
Percentage contribution: F

The calculation of Final course score is proposed as follows:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
GAA = Average score in First 9 weekly graded assignments

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
Qz1 = score in Quiz I (0, if not attempted)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
Qz2 = score in Quiz II (0, if not attempted)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
F = score in final exam

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
NPPE=Non-proctored programming assignment ( NPPE1 21st Nov to 24th Nov

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
NPPE2 11th Dec to 14th Dec )

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
Overall score for eligible students:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 13.Introduction to Deep Learning and Generative AI (DA2001)]
Final course score T = 0.1 GAA + 0.2 Qz1 + 0.2 Qz2 + 0.25 F + 0.1 NPPE1 + 0.15 NPPE2

Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
Here are the timelines for students doing projects in the SEP 2026  terms who will be entering Degree level in the Jan 2027 term. Without completing the 12 courses and 4 projects, you cannot start degree level courses from Jan 2027 onwards.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
For entering the Degree level in JAN 2027, you have  to complete ALL projects and vivas by Dec 2026. `

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
BDM project final submission has to be done and approved by Oct/Nov/Dec 2026 so that viva can be over in   Oct/Nov/Dec 2026. Project grade for all the courses will be pushed on/before  Jan 15th. Students are requested to complete the projects at least two terms before their diploma completion term i.e DO NOT REGISTER OR HAVE PROJECTS ALONE LEFT IN YOUR LAST TERM OF DIPLOMA.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
Very important:

[TYPE: ordered_list]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
1. Viva policy for MLP, DL&GEN AI , Viva Policy for Appdev

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
Students who completed App dev1, App dev2, MLP and BDM in Sep 2021/Jan 2022 terms:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
If you complete the 6 courses and 2 projects of any of the two Diplomas, you will get the Diploma certificates. But your credits will not be 27 for this Diploma but 23 or 25 depending on how many of these courses were completed in these 2 terms. The projects done as part of the courses of Sep 2021/Jan 2022 will not get the 2 credits. So there is no issue with the Diploma level.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Timelines (Appdev1, Appdev2 & MLP, BDM projects):]
When you come to the BSc level, 114 credits are required to obtain the BSc certificate. Hence this deficit of 2/4/6/8 credits will have to be made up by taking more elective courses offered by the IITM BS program.

[TYPE: heading]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Degree Level courses

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Level of the course:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
The first digit of the 4 digits given in the course code represents the level of the course.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Eg: CT is CS1001 is a 1 level course.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Deep Learning CS3004 is a 3 level course.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Industry 4.0 MS4001 is a 4 level course.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
BSc level:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
It is now mandatory that students have to complete both the core pairs and SPG in the BSc level. Credits = 20

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
All remaining 8 credits can be earned from IITM BS courses or NPTEL (maximum upto 4 credits from Table 2 ) or if there is an option to credit campus courses (IITM or other institutes), that too can be done.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
BS level:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
It is implemented wef Sep 2023 for the batch that enters the degree level in Sep 2023.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
2 courses in the level 4 or higher should be mandatorily completed in each of the BP and BD categories. Please refer course category in table1

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Out of the remaining 12 c redits, it is MANDATORY to earn 4 credits in the HS/MG category. HS/MG can come from inhouse electives or from NPTEL-Table 3 .

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Remaining credits are from any of the other in-house/campus electives or Apprenticeship electives.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Apprenticeship:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Apprenticeship is completely optional. Students can also complete the BS level by just doing only course work.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
Academic policies

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
1. In each programming assignment, be it any course or any OPPE,  taking help from LLMs (e.g. ChatGPT, Gemini) partially or completely is considered plagiarism.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
2. Unless explicitly permitted, do not use LLMs. Using LLMs is considered a violation of honour code.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
3. Students can discuss and learn from each other but the assignments are expected to be done individually based on their understanding.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: Degree Level courses]
4. Please do not share your assignments with others before the deadlines. If similarities are found between submissions, all will be penalised irrespective of who did it first and who shared it with whom.

1. Software Testing (CS3002)

[TYPE: paragraph]

Above to be attended in person at designated centres.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
Eligibility to get final course grade: Attending the End sem exam

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
Overall score for eligible students:

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
GAA = Average score in First 10 weekly graded assignments

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
Qz1 = score in Quiz I (0, if not attempted)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
Qz2 = score in Quiz II (0, if not attempted)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
F - score in End Term exam

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 1. Software Testing (CS3002)]
T = 0.1GAA + 0.4F + 0.25Qz1 + 0.25Qz2

4. AI: Search Methods for Proble m Solving (CS3003)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 4. AI: Search Methods for Proble m Solving (CS3003)]
Academic policies

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 4. AI: Search Methods for Proble m Solving (CS3003)]
1. In each programming assignment, be it any course or any OPPE,  taking help from LLMs (e.g. ChatGPT, Gemini) partially or completely is considered plagiarism.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 4. AI: Search Methods for Proble m Solving (CS3003)]
2. Unless explicitly permitted, do not use LLMs. Using LLMs is considered a violation of honour code.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 4. AI: Search Methods for Proble m Solving (CS3003)]
3. Students can discuss and learn from each other but the assignments are expected to be done individually based on their understanding.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 4. AI: Search Methods for Proble m Solving (CS3003)]
4. Please do not share your assignments with others before the deadlines. If similarities are found between submissions, all will be penalised irrespective of who did it first and who shared it with whom.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 6 . Programming in C (CS3005)]
Repeat_OPPE Criteria (w.e.f from Jan 2026 term) [updated on Jan 05, 2026]

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 6 . Programming in C (CS3005)]
Students who were OPPE eligible and received an I_OP in the May 2026 term, and have registered as Repeat OPPE for the Sep 2026 term, will continue to be considered eligible for OPPE in Sep 2026. However, students who were marked OPPE Ineligible with I_OP or I_Both and have registered for the respective course as Repeat OPPE or Repeat OPPE & End Term must regain OPPE eligibility in the Sep 2026 term by submitting the required assignments and OPPE SCT. If OPPE eligibility is not attained, the OPPE will not be scheduled and a U grade will be awarded for the Sep 2026 term.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 6 . Programming in C (CS3005)]
Final course score calculation:

[TYPE: unordered_list]
[SECTION: 6 . Programming in C (CS3005)]
- GAA =  Average score in First 10 weekly graded assignments(GA+GrPAs)
- Qz1 = score in Quiz 1 (0, if not attempted) - in centre
- OPPE1 = score in OPPE 1 (0, if not attempted) - programming exam 1
- OPPE2 = score in OPPE 2 (0, if not attempted) - programming exam 2
- F = score in final End Term exam

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 6 . Programming in C (CS3005)]
T = 0.10GAA (GA+GrPAs) + 0.20Qz1 + 0.20 OPPE1 + 0.20 OPPE2 + 0.30F

[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]
18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]
Academic policies

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]
1. In each programming assignment, be it any course or any OPPE,  taking help from LLMs (e.g. ChatGPT, Gemini) partially or completely is considered plagiarism.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]
2. Unless explicitly permitted, do not use LLMs. Using LLMs is considered a violation of honour code.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]
3. Students can discuss and learn from each other but the assignments are expected to be done individually based on their understanding.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]
4. Please do not share your assignments with others before the deadlines. If similarities are found between submissions, all will be penalised irrespective of who did it first and who shared it with whom.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]

[SECTION: 18. Design Thinking for Data-Driven App Development (MS4002)]

Academic policies

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 20. Introduction to Big Data  (DA5001)]
1. In each programming assignment, be it any course or any OPPE,  taking help from LLMs (e.g. ChatGPT, Gemini) partially or completely is considered plagiarism.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 20. Introduction to Big Data  (DA5001)]
2. Unless explicitly permitted, do not use LLMs. Using LLMs is considered a violation of honour code.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 20. Introduction to Big Data  (DA5001)]
3. Students can discuss and learn from each other but the assignments are expected to be done individually based on their understanding.

[TYPE: paragraph]
[SECTION: 20. Introduction to Big Data  (DA5001)]
4. Please do not share your assignments with others before the deadlines. If similarities are found between submissions, all will be penalised irrespective of who did it first and who shared it with whom.
